# File 001: Project mission and scope

**Assigned team member:** Member 1  
**Document type:** governance  
**Primary evidence label:** PROJECT DECISION

## Why this topic matters for SIH26079

This brief isolates one question: **what must the team understand or decide about project mission and scope before the project can be built, tested, presented, or maintained?** Keeping the question narrow prevents the study pack from becoming 120 invented papers. It also gives Member 1 a concrete handoff that can be checked against the master documentation.

## Learning objective

State what the package says SIH26079 is intended to accomplish and where the brief must stop. The expected result is a traceable understanding of the topic, not a claim that an implementation, experiment, or operational validation already exists.

**Truth boundary.** The specification requires the current project to be treated as a public-proxy prototype using GEFS or WeatherBench 2 with ERA5 reference analysis. NCMRWF/NEPS operational validation is future and conditional until paired historical data, metadata, permission, and a verification source are actually provided. No supplied material establishes that a SIH26079 model has been trained or evaluated.

## Key concepts in simple language

A **research study file** is an internal learning brief. It may summarize confirmed literature, record a project decision, define a target or acceptance gate, expose an unverified gap, or describe future work. These categories are not interchangeable. A target is a condition to test later. A project decision is a chosen design rule. A gap is information the team still needs. A future item is outside the current demonstrated scope.

For this topic, the team should begin by asking what is observable in the supplied records, what is only proposed, and what would require a new source or experiment. The safe default is to preserve uncertainty rather than fill it with a plausible-sounding detail. A source name in the specification is a lead unless the project records confirm the exact statement.

## Project-specific decisions or implementation rules

1. Keep the topic tied to its stated role in the SIH26079 curriculum.
2. Do not turn an acceptance gate into an achieved result.
3. Do not infer access, implementation status, official wording, citations, figures, or performance from the existence of a plan.
4. Link the work to the fixed file IDs below so later members can review the same evidence trail.

The immediate topic rule is: **Project mission and scope must be handled as project decision unless a named project source upgrades or changes that status.**

## Evidence and source notes

The available source for this generated package is the user-supplied specification, especially **Specification: Objective; Group A**. The specification names the consolidated master document and additional project files as the intended primary and supporting sources, but those files were not present in the workspace at generation time. Therefore, this brief does not pretend to quote or verify their contents. When the project documents are supplied, replace this source note with the exact filename, heading, page or section, and any confirmed external reference.

The package-level source hierarchy is: the consolidated master documentation first; original attachments and archive second for traceability and detail; verified references third; and the specification as the current available scope and truth-boundary record. Extended-library items remain research leads unless confirmed in project records. The withdrawn Hurricane Michael article must not be used as standing evidence. Any file that depends on the IFS data-assimilation PDF must retain its partial-extraction warning.

## What is confirmed, what is proposed, and what is not yet verified

| Status | Treatment in this brief |
|---|---|
| Confirmed by available specification | The curriculum placement, required evidence discipline, and public-proxy/NCMRWF boundary. |
| Proposed or project decision | The topic-specific rule or workflow described above. |
| Target / acceptance gate | A condition to test or document later; never a result. |
| Unverified / gap | Exact claims, citations, data access, implementation status, and empirical findings absent from the available records. |
| Future / out of scope | Operational NCMRWF/NEPS validation or any other extension not supported by paired data, metadata, permission, and verification evidence. |

## Practical task or study exercise

Write a one-paragraph scope statement using only the supplied specification. The member should record the source location, decision, unresolved question, and evidence status in the team question register. If the named source cannot be found, the correct outcome is **UNVERIFIED / GAP**, not a reconstructed answer.

## Questions to bring to the team discussion

1. Which exact source section confirms the topic, and is it literature, a project decision, a target, a limitation, a gap, or future work?
2. What would count as acceptable evidence before this topic can be presented as complete?
3. Does the topic accidentally depend on information available only after the forecast issue time?
4. Does any wording imply an achieved result, causal explanation, universal conformal guarantee, or NCMRWF operational validation?
5. Which owner should update this brief when the master documentation changes?

## Deliverable or handoff after study

A scope statement with explicit inclusions and exclusions. The handoff must include a short conclusion, evidence label, source location or missing-source note, one decision or open question, and links to the prerequisite and next files.

## Dependencies and next files to read

**Prerequisites:** None; begin with the package README..  
**Suggested next step:** Read the next numbered file in the curriculum, then follow any domain-specific dependency in the master index. This file is intentionally cross-linked rather than repeating material from neighboring studies.

## Citation and terminology notes

Use the package labels exactly: **CONFIRMED**, **FROM LITERATURE**, **PROJECT DECISION**, **UNVERIFIED / GAP**, **FUTURE / OUT OF SCOPE**, and **TARGET / ACCEPTANCE GATE**. Cite only confirmed references. If a citation is merely a library lead, say so explicitly and do not use it as standing evidence. “Prototype” means the public-proxy research scope described by the specification; it does not mean trained, evaluated, deployed, or operationally validated.

Preserve the non-negotiable statement that the current prototype uses public proxies and NCMRWF validation is future and conditional. The expected result is a traceable understanding of the topic, not a claim that an implementation, experiment, or operational validation already exists.

**Truth boundary.** The specification requires the current project to be treated as a public-proxy prototype using GEFS or WeatherBench 2 with ERA5 reference analysis. No supplied material establishes that a SIH26079 model has been trained or evaluated.

It may summarize confirmed literature, record a project decision, define a target or acceptance gate, expose an unverified gap, or describe future work. These categories are not interchangeable. A target is a condition to test later. A project decision is a chosen design rule. A gap is information the team still needs. A future item is outside the current demonstrated scope.

For this topic, the team should begin by asking what is observable in the supplied records, what is only proposed, and what would require a new source or experiment. The safe default is to preserve uncertainty rather than fill it with a plausible-sounding detail. A source name in the specification is a lead unless the project records confirm the exact statement.

## Project-specific decisions or implementation rules

1. Keep the topic tied to its stated role in the SIH26079 curriculum.
2. Do not turn an acceptance gate into an achieved result.
3. Do not infer access, implementation status, official wording, citations, figures, or performance from the existence of a plan.
4. Link the work to the fixed file IDs below so later members can review the same evidence trail.

The immediate topic rule is: **GEFS ingestion planning must be handled as project decision unless a named project source upgrades or changes that status.**

# File 044: Ensemble member completeness

**Assigned team member:** Member 2  
**Document type:** data  
**Primary evidence label:** TARGET / ACCEPTANCE GATE

## Why this topic matters for SIH26079

This brief isolates one question: **what must the team understand or decide about ensemble member completeness before the project can be built, tested, presented, or maintained?** Keeping the question narrow prevents the study pack from becoming 120 invented papers. It also gives Member 2 a concrete handoff that can be checked against the master documentation.

## Learning objective

Treat missing ensemble members as a quality gate rather than silently filling them. The expected result is a traceable understanding of the topic, not a claim that an implementation, experiment, or operational validation already exists.

**Truth boundary.** The specification requires the current project to be treated as a public-proxy prototype using GEFS or WeatherBench 2 with ERA5 reference analysis. NCMRWF/NEPS operational validation is future and conditional until paired historical data, metadata, permission, and a verification source are actually provided. No supplied material establishes that a SIH26079 model has been trained or evaluated.

## Key concepts in simple language

A **research study file** is an internal learning brief. It may summarize confirmed literature, record a project decision, define a target or acceptance gate, expose an unverified gap, or describe future work. These categories are not interchangeable. A target is a condition to test later. A project decision is a chosen design rule. A gap is information the team still needs. A future item is outside the current demonstrated scope.

For this topic, the team should begin by asking what is observable in the supplied records, what is only proposed, and what would require a new source or experiment. The safe default is to preserve uncertainty rather than fill it with a plausible-sounding detail. A source name in the specification is a lead unless the project records confirm the exact statement.

## Project-specific decisions or implementation rules

1. Keep the topic tied to its stated role in the SIH26079 curriculum.
2. Do not turn an acceptance gate into an achieved result.
3. Do not infer access, implementation status, official wording, citations, figures, or performance from the existence of a plan.
4. Link the work to the fixed file IDs below so later members can review the same evidence trail.

The immediate topic rule is: **Ensemble member completeness must be handled as target / acceptance gate unless a named project source upgrades or changes that status.**

## Evidence and source notes

The available source for this generated package is the user-supplied specification, especially **Specification: Group E**. The specification names the consolidated master document and additional project files as the intended primary and supporting sources, but those files were not present in the workspace at generation time. Therefore, this brief does not pretend to quote or verify their contents. When the project documents are supplied, replace this source note with the exact filename, heading, page or section, and any confirmed external reference.

The package-level source hierarchy is: the consolidated master documentation first; original attachments and archive second for traceability and detail; verified references third; and the specification as the current available scope and truth-boundary record. Extended-library items remain research leads unless confirmed in project records. The withdrawn Hurricane Michael article must not be used as standing evidence. Any file that depends on the IFS data-assimilation PDF must retain its partial-extraction warning.

## What is confirmed, what is proposed, and what is not yet verified

| Status | Treatment in this brief |
|---|---|
| Confirmed by available specification | The curriculum placement, required evidence discipline, and public-proxy/NCMRWF boundary. |
| Proposed or project decision | The topic-specific rule or workflow described above. |
| Target / acceptance gate | A condition to test or document later; never a result. |
| Unverified / gap | Exact claims, citations, data access, implementation status, and empirical findings absent from the available records. |
| Future / out of scope | Operational NCMRWF/NEPS validation or any other extension not supported by paired data, metadata, permission, and verification evidence. |

## Practical task or study exercise

Define completeness checks and escalation behavior. The member should record the source location, decision, unresolved question, and evidence status in the team question register. If the named source cannot be found, the correct outcome is **UNVERIFIED / GAP**, not a reconstructed answer.

## Questions to bring to the team discussion

1. Which exact source section confirms the topic, and is it literature, a project decision, a target, a limitation, a gap, or future work?
2. What would count as acceptable evidence before this topic can be presented as complete?
3. Does the topic accidentally depend on information available only after the forecast issue time?
4. Does any wording imply an achieved result, causal explanation, universal conformal guarantee, or NCMRWF operational validation?
5. Which owner should update this brief when the master documentation changes?

## Deliverable or handoff after study

An ensemble QC gate. The handoff must include a short conclusion, evidence label, source location or missing-source note, one decision or open question, and links to the prerequisite and next files.

## Dependencies and next files to read

**Prerequisites:** 041,043.  
**Suggested next step:** Read the next numbered file in the curriculum, then follow any domain-specific dependency in the master index. This file is intentionally cross-linked rather than repeating material from neighboring studies.

## Citation and terminology notes

Use the package labels exactly: **CONFIRMED**, **FROM LITERATURE**, **PROJECT DECISION**, **UNVERIFIED / GAP**, **FUTURE / OUT OF SCOPE**, and **TARGET / ACCEPTANCE GATE**. Cite only confirmed references. If a citation is merely a library lead, say so explicitly and do not use it as standing evidence. “Prototype” means the public-proxy research scope described by the specification; it does not mean trained, evaluated, deployed, or operationally validated.

# File 045: Missingness and stale-data policy

**Assigned team member:** Member 3  
**Document type:** operations  
**Primary evidence label:** PROJECT DECISION

## Why this topic matters for SIH26079

045 ek aisi boundary hai jo usable input aur us input ke beech jahan silently forecast-bust decision corrupt ho sakta hai. Missing values download failure, partial ensemble, unavailable variable, ya join jiska koi matching record nahi nikla se aa sakte hain. Stale value alag cheez hai: woh maujood hota hai, lekin uska timestamp forecast issue time se purana hota hai ya expected refresh interval violate karta hai. System ko in conditions ko ordinary numeric values ki tarah treat nahi karna chahiye.

## Learning objective

Is brief ke end tak, team plain language mein topic explain kar paayegi, identify kar paayegi ki kaunsa project decision ya research question is topic se control hota hai, aur bata payegi ki kaunsi evidence chahiye hogi stronger claim karne se pehle. Ye file ek focused internal study brief hai, published research paper nahi aur na hi koi evidence hai ki implementation ya experiment complete hua hai.

## Key concepts in simple language

Explicit states define karo jaise COMPLETE, DEGRADED, REJECTED, aur UNKNOWN. COMPLETE ka matlab hai required fields, timestamps, units, aur ensemble membership validation pass karte hain. DEGRADED ka matlab hai documented optional field absent hai lekin configured model contract phir bhi processing allow karta hai. REJECTED ka matlab hai required field, time key, coordinate, ya ensemble condition fail ho gaya. UNKNOWN ka matlab hai system freshness ya provenance establish nahi kar pa raha. Har state data aur prediction envelope ke saath travel karna chahiye.

Key distinction hai between availability, validity, and use. Availability puchta hai kya value declared prediction time par exist karti hai. Validity puchta hai kya uska timestamp, units, coordinates, provenance, aur quality checks pass karte hain. Use puchta hai kya project contract value ko training, evaluation, replay, ya inference mein permit karta hai. Ek value available ho sakti hai lekin invalid, valid ho sakti hai lekin kisi particular task ke liye forbidden, ya absent ho sakti hai aur isliye unsafe to infer.

Truth boundary. Current project ek public-proxy prototype hai jo GEFS ya WeatherBench 2 use karta hai with ERA5 reference analysis. NCMRWF/NEPS operational validation future aur conditional hai jab tak paired historical data, metadata, permission, aur verification source actually provide nahi kiye jaate. Koi supplied material establish nahi karta ki SIH26079 model train ya evaluate hua hai.

Implementation caution. Specification se named source documents ko check karna zaroori hai before kisi proposed threshold, schema, ya status ko implemented project fact treat karne se pehle.

## Project-specific decisions or implementation rules

1. Preserve issue time, valid time, lead time, dataset version, model version, aur provenance ko explicit fields ke roop mein rakho.  
2. Fail closed jab required timestamp, source identifier, checksum, ya quality state missing ho.  
3. Public-proxy prototype ko kisi bhi future operational NCMRWF/NEPS pathway se separate rakho.  
4. Targets aur acceptance gates ko visibly distinct rakho from achieved measurements.  
5. Topic ko prerequisite files se link karo instead of unke content ko is brief mein copy karne ke.

Is file ke working decision yeh hai ki missingness and stale-data policy ko project decision maana jayega jab tak consolidated master document aur source archive koi specific rule confirm nahi karte.

## Evidence and source notes

Available source anchor user-supplied specification hai, khaaskar required Group E aur Group F curriculum entry is file ke liye. Named consolidated master document aur supporting attachments generation workspace mein present nahi the, isliye exact section names, citations, data-access facts, aur implementation status abhi verify karne baaki hain. Ye file jaan-boojh kar unko manufacture nahi karti.

Requested source hierarchy hai: `SIH26079_MASTER_PROJECT_DOCUMENTATION_MERGED.md` as primary; original attachments aur archive for traceability and detail; verified references; aur completed master documentation. Extended library items research leads hi rahenge unless project records mein confirm ho. Withdrawn Hurricane Michael article standing evidence nahi hai. Agar future revision IFS data-assimilation PDF par depend karta hai, toh uska partial-extraction warning claim ke saath attach rehna chahiye.

## What is confirmed, what is proposed, and what is not yet verified

| Category | Treatment for this file |
|---|---|
| Confirmed by the available specification | This topic is required in the SIH26079 curriculum and must be handled with explicit evidence labels. |
| Project decision or proposed rule | The topic-specific workflow and safeguards described above. |
| Target / acceptance gate | A condition that must be tested or documented later; it is not a result. |
| Unverified / gap | Exact source wording, project implementation status, data access, empirical performance, and any claim not present in the available specification. |
| Future / out of scope | Operational validation or stronger scientific claims requiring new data, permission, metadata, or verification evidence. |

## Practical task or study exercise

Ek table banao jisme ek row ho for missing forecast field, missing ensemble member, stale cycle, failed checksum, aur absent reference analysis. Har row ke liye detection, state, whether inference is allowed, UI wording, aur owner specify karo. Table ko invented thresholds se fill mat karo; unko configuration decisions mark karo.

Exercise ko ek chhota artifact ke roop mein complete karo: ek table, diagram, test case, protocol, ya decision record. Include karo ek source column aur evidence-status column. Agar koi named project source nahi milta, toh likho **UNVERIFIED / GAP** aur item ko shared question register mein add karo instead of filling it with a plausible assumption.

## Questions to bring to the team discussion

1. Sabse chhota precise rule kya hai jo ensure karega ki yeh topic leakage, ambiguity, ya unsupported claim cause na kare?  
2. Kaunse fields, timestamps, versions, ya source sections auditability ke liye retain karne zaroori hain?  
3. Jab required evidence ya input missing ho to failure-safe behavior kya hoga?  
4. Kaunsa later experiment ya acceptance gate propose rule ko confirm ya reject kar sakta hai?  
5. Kya koi wording galti se trained result, operational NCMRWF validation, causal explanation, ya universal guarantee imply kar rahi hai?

## Deliverable or handoff after study

Handoff karo completed exercise, ek three-sentence conclusion, evidence label, exact source location agar baad mein verify hua, aur ek unresolved question. Handoff aisa hona chahiye ki next dependent file use use kar sake bina poore topic ko dobara padhe.

# File 046: Data lifecycle states

**Assigned team member:** Member 3  
**Document type:** governance  
**Primary evidence label:** PROJECT DECISION

## Why this topic matters for SIH26079

Ek data file automatically trustworthy nahi hoti sirf isliye ki wo successfully download ho gayi. Ek lifecycle state record karta hai ki artifact ke saath kya hua aur har point par kaunsa evidence maujood hai. Isse project ko bachav milta hai taaki koi discovered ya cached file ko aise use na kiya jaye jaise usne validation pass kar li ho.

## Learning objective

Is brief ke end tak, team plain language mein topic samjha sakti honi chahiye, identify kar sakti honi chahiye ki ye kaunsa project decision ya research question control karta hai, aur bata sakti honi chahiye ki ek stronger claim karne se pehle kaunsa evidence chahiye hoga. Ye file ek focused internal study brief hai, published research paper nahi, aur na hi ye evidence hai ki implementation ya experiment complete hua hai.

## Key concepts in simple language

Ek chhota state machine use karo: DISCOVERED, DOWNLOADED, CHECKSUMMED, PARSED, HARMONIZED, QUALITY_CHECKED, FEATURE_READY, LABEL_READY, ARCHIVED, and QUARANTINED. Har transition ke saath actor ya process, timestamp, source identifier, checksum, aur reason hona chahiye. QUARANTINED ek terminal safety state hai jab tak explicit review artifact ko reopen na kare. Ye state artifact version ka hai, sirf filename ka nahi.

Key farq ye hai ki **availability**, **validity**, aur **use** ke beech. Availability yeh poochti hai ki kya koi value declared prediction time par exist karti hai. Validity yeh poochti hai ki uska timestamp, units, coordinates, provenance, aur quality checks pass karte hain ya nahi. Use yeh poochta hai ki kya project contract us value ko training, evaluation, replay, ya inference mein allow karta hai. Ek value available ho sakti hai lekin invalid, valid ho sakti hai lekin kisi particular task ke liye forbidden, ya absent ho sakti hai aur isliye infer karna unsafe hai.

**Truth boundary.** Current project ek public-proxy prototype hai jo GEFS ya WeatherBench 2 ke saath ERA5 reference analysis use karta hai. NCMRWF/NEPS operational validation future ka aur conditional hai jab tak paired historical data, metadata, permission, aur verification source actually provide na kiye jaayein. Koi bhi supplied material ye establish nahi karta ki SIH26079 model train ya evaluate hua hai.

**Implementation caution.** Specification mein naam diye gaye source documents ko check karna zaroori hai pehle ki kisi bhi proposed threshold, schema, ya status ko implemented project fact maana jaye.

## Project-specific decisions or implementation rules

1. Preserve issue time, valid time, lead time, dataset version, model version, aur provenance as explicit fields.  
2. Fail closed jab required timestamp, source identifier, checksum, ya quality state missing ho.  
3. Public-proxy prototype ko future operational NCMRWF/NEPS pathway se alag rakho.  
4. Targets aur acceptance gates ko achieved measurements se visibly distinct rakho.  
5. Topic ko prerequisite files se link karo na ki unka content is brief mein copy karo.

Is file ke liye working decision ye hai ki **data lifecycle states** ko **project decision** treat kiya jaaye jab tak consolidated master document aur source archive kisi zyada specific rule ko confirm nahi kar dete.

## Evidence and source notes

Available source anchor user-supplied specification hai, khaaskar required Group E aur Group F curriculum entry is file ke liye. Named consolidated master document aur supporting attachments generation workspace mein maujood nahi the, isliye exact section names, citations, data-access facts, aur implementation status verify karne baaki hain. Ye file jaan-bujh kar unko manufacture nahi karti.

## Practical task or study exercise

Draw karo transitions aur list karo evidence jo inke beech move karne ke liye required hai. Ek failed checksum, ek incomplete download, ek unit mismatch, aur ek corrected metadata record include karo. Dikhao kaunse states training inputs ke roop mein allowed hain aur kaunse nahi.

# File 047: Allowed issue-time features

**Niyukt team member:** Member 3  
**Document ka type:** ML  
**Primary evidence label:** PROJECT DECISION

## Why this topic matters for SIH26079

Ek bust detector sirf wahi information use kar sakta hai jo forecast issue hone ke time available ho. Isliye issue-time rule har feature ke liye central admissibility test hai. Koi field scientific roop se useful ho sakti hai aur phir bhi illegal ho sakti hai agar woh later observations ya kisi reference analysis se generate hui ho jo issue time par available nahi thi.

## Learning objective

Is brief ke end tak, team plain language mein topic explain kar paani chahiye, identify kar sakni chahiye ki kaunsa project decision ya research question isse controlled hai, aur bata sakni chahiye ki stronger daave se pehle kaun sa evidence chahiye. Yeh file ek focused internal study brief hai, published research paper nahi hai aur na hi yeh proof hai ki implementation ya experiment complete hai.

## Key concepts in simple language

Har candidate feature ke liye, uska source, issue timestamp, valid-time coverage, transformation, aur availability flag store karo. Allowed features mein forecast-state values aur issue-time ensemble geometry shamil ho sakte hain jab source contract confirm kare ki woh available hain. Reference-analysis values, realized observations, post-event error, aur future cycle revisions prediction inputs ke roop mein allowed nahi hain unless koi alag task explicitly unhe historical context ke roop mein define kare jo issue time par available ho.

Key distinction availability, validity, aur use ke beech hai. Availability poochta hai kya koi value declared prediction time par exist karti thi. Validity poochta hai kya uska timestamp, units, coordinates, provenance, aur quality checks pass karte hain. Use poochta hai kya project contract us value ko training, evaluation, replay, ya inference mein use karne ki ijazat deta hai. Ek value available ho sakti hai lekin invalid ho, valid ho lekin kisi particular task ke liye forbidden ho, ya absent ho aur isliye unsafe ho infer karna.

**Sachai ki seema.** Current project ek public-proxy prototype use kar raha hai GEFS ya WeatherBench 2 ke saath aur ERA5 reference analysis. NCMRWF/NEPS operational validation future ka aur conditional hai jab tak paired historical data, metadata, permission, aur verification source actually provide na ho. Koi supplied material yeh establish nahi karta ki SIH26079 model train ya evaluate hua hai.

**Implementation caution.** Specification mein naam wale source documents ko check karna zaroori hai before kisi proposed threshold, schema, ya status ko implemented project fact maan ne ke.

## Project-specific decisions or implementation rules

1. Issue time, valid time, lead time, dataset version, model version, aur provenance ko explicit fields ke roop mein preserve karo.  
2. Jab required timestamp, source identifier, checksum, ya quality state missing ho to fail closed karo.  
3. Public-proxy prototype ko future operational NCMRWF/NEPS pathway se alag rakho.  
4. Targets aur acceptance gates ko achieved measurements se visibly alag rakho.  
5. Topic ko prerequisite files se link karo, unka content is brief mein copy mat karo.

# File 048: Forbidden future features

**Assigned team member:** Member 3  
**Document type:** ML  
**Primary evidence label:** PROJECT DECISION

## Why this topic matters for SIH26079

Leakage sirf observation naam ki column tak simit nahi hai. Yeh joins, normalization, imputation, target construction, feature selection, caching, ya koi reference field jo issue time ke baad banaya gaya ho, in sab ke through aa sakta hai. Ek model strong dikh sakta hai jab woh aisi information learn kar raha ho jo prediction time par maujood nahi hogi.

## Learning objective

Is brief ke end tak, team plain language mein topic explain kar paani chahiye, identify karna chahiye ki yeh project decision ya research question ko kaunse hisse pe control karta hai, aur batana chahiye ki stronger claim banane se pehle kaunse evidence ki zarurat hogi. Yeh file ek focused internal study brief hai, published research paper nahi, aur yeh saboot nahi hai ki implementation ya experiment complete hai.

## Key concepts in simple language

Realized error, ERA5/reference values at future valid times, labels, post-event quality flags, future cycle revisions, aur validation ya test periods se fitted statistics ko forbid karo. Derived features un timestamps ko inherit karte hain jo unke computation mein last use huye the. Jab timestamps missing ho to feature pipeline ko fail closed karna chahiye, assume karke admissibility pe proceed mat karo.

Key distinction availability, validity, aur use ke beech hai. Availability yeh puchti hai ki kya koi value declared prediction time par exist karti hai. Validity yeh puchti hai ki kya uska timestamp, units, coordinates, provenance, aur quality checks pass karte hain. Use yeh puchti hai ki kya project contract us value ko training, evaluation, replay, ya inference mein allow karta hai. Ek value available ho sakti hai lekin invalid, valid ho sakti hai lekin kisi particular task ke liye forbidden, ya absent ho sakti hai aur isliye unsafe to infer.

**Truth boundary.** Current project ek public-proxy prototype hai jo GEFS ya WeatherBench 2 use karta hai with ERA5 reference analysis. NCMRWF/NEPS operational validation future aur conditional hai jab tak paired historical data, metadata, permission, aur ek verification source actually provide nahi kiya jaata. Koi supplied material yeh establish nahi karta ki SIH26079 model ko train ya evaluate kiya gaya hai.

**Implementation caution.** Specification ke naam wale source documents ko check karna zaroori hai before kisi proposed threshold, schema, ya status ko implemented project fact maan ne se pehle.

## Project-specific decisions or implementation rules

1. Preserve issue time, valid time, lead time, dataset version, model version, aur provenance ko explicit fields ke roop mein rakho.
2. Jab required timestamp, source identifier, checksum, ya quality state missing ho to fail closed karo.
3. Public-proxy prototype ko kisi bhi future operational NCMRWF/NEPS pathway se separate rakho.
4. Targets aur acceptance gates ko achieved measurements se visibly distinct rakho.

**Primary evidence label:** FROM LITERATURE

## Why this topic matters for SIH26079

RMSE emphasizes large errors because the residual is squared before averaging. That may be useful for severe misses, but it can also make a small number of extremes dominate. MAE is easier to interpret in the original units and is less sensitive to outliers. Other measures may capture relative error, spatial displacement, or probabilistic quality. The project must choose a measure that matches the bust definition rather than choosing a familiar formula by habit.

## Learning objective

Is brief ke end tak, team ko ye bata paana chahiye ki topic ko simple language mein kaise samjhaya ja sakta hai, kaunsa project decision ya research question iske under aata hai, aur stronger claim karne se pehle kaunsa evidence zaroori hoga. Ye file ek focused internal study brief hai, published research paper nahi, aur na hi ye proof hai ki implementation ya experiment complete hai.

## Key concepts in simple language

Measure, units, aggregation, extremes ke prati sensitivity, aur woh scientific question jo woh answer karta hai — in sab ko record karo. Agar label kisi error distribution ke high quantile par based hai, toh woh ek proposed project choice hai, measured discovery nahi. Agar multiple measures rakhe ja rahe hain, toh unko version karo aur explain karo kaunsa primary hai, kaunsa sensitivity analysis ke liye hai, aur kaunsa diagnostic ke liye.

Key distinction hai between availability, validity, aur use. Availability poochta hai kya ek value declared prediction time par exist karti hai. Validity poochta hai kya uska timestamp, units, coordinates, provenance, aur quality checks pass karte hain. Use poochta hai kya project contract us value ko training, evaluation, replay, ya inference mein allow karta hai. Ek value available ho sakti hai par invalid, valid ho sakti hai par kisi particular task ke liye forbidden, ya absent aur isliye unsafe to infer.

**Truth boundary.** Current project ek public-proxy prototype hai jo GEFS ya WeatherBench 2 with ERA5 reference analysis use karta hai. NCMRWF/NEPS operational validation future aur conditional hai jab tak paired historical data, metadata, permission, aur ek verification source actually provide nahi kiya jaata. Koi bhi supplied material ye establish nahi karta ki SIH26079 model ko train ya evaluate kiya gaya hai.

**Result-status rule.** Koi bhi numeric comparison, prevalence, threshold choice, ya metric value ek later experiment record mein belong karti hai. Is brief mein koi SIH26079 result nahi hai.

## Project-specific decisions or implementation rules

1. Preserve issue time, valid time, lead time, dataset version, model version, aur provenance ko explicit fields ke roop mein rakho.
2. Jab required timestamp, source identifier, checksum, ya quality state missing ho toh fail closed karo.
3. Public-proxy prototype ko kisi bhi future operational NCMRWF/NEPS pathway se separate rakho.
4. Targets aur acceptance gates ko achieved measurements se visibly distinct rakho.
5. Topic ko prerequisite files se link karo bajaye unka content is brief mein copy karne ke.

# File 053: Training-only robust normalization

**Assigned team member:** Member 3  
**Document type:** ML  
**Primary evidence label:** PROJECT DECISION

## Why this topic matters for SIH26079

Normalization ek model ko dikhaye jaane wale scale ko badalti hai. Agar iske statistics validation ya test periods se compute kiye gaye hain to ye information leak bhi kar sakti hai. Robust normalization aam taur par un statistics ko use karti hai jo extreme values ke liye kam sensitive hote hain, lekin exact estimator aur feature scope ko evaluation se pehle fix aur record karna zaroori hai.

## Learning objective

Is brief ke ant tak, team ko ye batana aana chahiye ki topic ko plain language mein kaise samjhaya jaaye, kaunsa project decision ya research question is topic ke control mein hai, aur kis tarah ka evidence chahiye hoga tab tak jab tak hum koi stronger claim na karein. Ye file ek focused internal study brief hai — published research paper nahi, aur na hi ye saboot hai ki implementation ya experiment complete ho chuka hai.

## Key concepts in simple language

Har transform ko sirf training data par fit karo, uske parameters ko model version ke saath store karo, aur wahi parameters validation, test, replay, aur inference data par apply karo. Missing values ko declared transform ke andar ya usse pehle handle karna zaroori hai. Agar transform ko har evaluation period ke liye alag se fit kiya gaya to comparisons invalid ho jaate hain, jab tak experiment explicitly us behavior ko study na kare.

Mukhya farq ye hai ki hum availability, validity, aur use ko alag kar rahe hain. Availability puchti hai ki kya koi value declared prediction time par maujood hai. Validity puchti hai ki kya uska timestamp, units, coordinates, provenance, aur quality checks pass hote hain. Use puchta hai ki kya project contract us value ko training, evaluation, replay, ya inference mein use karne ki ijazat deta hai. Ek value available ho sakti hai lekin invalid; valid ho sakti hai lekin kisi khaas task ke liye forbidden; ya absent ho sakti hai aur isliye inference ke liye unsafe ho.

**Sachai ki boundary.** Current project ek public-proxy prototype hai jo GEFS ya WeatherBench 2 ko ERA5 reference analysis ke saath use karta hai. NCMRWF/NEPS ka operational validation future ka aur conditional hai jab tak paired historical data, metadata, permission, aur ek verification source waqayi provide na kiya jaaye. Koi bhi supplied material ye establish nahi karta ki SIH26079 model ko train ya evaluate kiya ja chuka hai.

**Implementation caution.** Specification dwara named source documents ko check karna zaroori hai pehle ki kisi proposed threshold, schema, ya status ko implemented project fact maan liya jaaye.

# File 056: Ambiguity and gray-band handling

**Assigned team member:** Member 3  
**Document type:** ML  
**Primary evidence label:** PROJECT DECISION

## Why this topic matters for SIH26079

Ek hard threshold do lagbhag identical cases ko opposite labels de sakta hai. Ek gray band is ambiguity ko visible banata hai, instead of yeh pretend karne ke ki measurement, alignment, aur reference analysis exact hain. Band ko uncertain training examples ko exclude karne, unhe alag report karne, ya soft target banane ke liye use kiya ja sakta hai; choice ko explicit hona chahiye.

## Learning objective

Is brief ke end tak, team plain language mein topic samjha sakni chahiye, identify kar sakni chahiye ki kaunsa project decision ya research question isse control hota hai, aur bata sakni chahiye ki stronger claim karne se pehle kaunsa evidence chahiye hoga. Ye file ek focused internal study brief hai, published research paper nahi, aur na hi ye evidence hai ki implementation ya experiment complete hai.

## Key concepts in simple language

Gray band ko label threshold ke relative define karo aur specify karo ki yeh training, evaluation, ya dono ke dauran apply hota hai. Gray-band cases ko silently remove mat karo kyunki isse prevalence change hoti hai aur results biased ho sakte hain. Excluded ya ambiguous cases ki number aur distribution split aur event stratum ke hisaab se report karo.

Key distinction hai between availability, validity, aur use. Availability poochta hai kya koi value declared prediction time par exist karti hai. Validity poochta hai kya uska timestamp, units, coordinates, provenance, aur quality checks pass karte hain. Use poochta hai kya project contract us value ko training, evaluation, replay, ya inference mein allow karta hai. Ek value available ho sakti hai lekin invalid; valid ho sakti hai lekin kisi particular task ke liye forbidden; ya absent ho sakti hai aur isliye infer karna unsafe hai.

**Truth boundary.** Current project ek public-proxy prototype use kar raha hai GEFS ya WeatherBench 2 with ERA5 reference analysis. NCMRWF/NEPS operational validation future mein conditional hai jab tak paired historical data, metadata, permission, aur verification source actually provide na kiye jaayen. Koi supplied material yeh establish nahi karta ki SIH26079 model train ya evaluate kiya gaya hai.

## Project-specific decisions or implementation rules

1. Preserve issue time, valid time, lead time, dataset version, model version, aur provenance ko explicit fields ke roop mein rakho.  
2. Fail closed jab required timestamp, source identifier, checksum, ya quality state missing ho.  
3. Public-proxy prototype ko future operational NCMRWF/NEPS pathway se separate rakho.  
4. Targets aur acceptance gates ko achieved measurements se visibly distinct rakho.  
5. Topic ko prerequisite files se link karo instead of unka content is brief mein copy karne ke.

Is file ka working decision yeh hai ki ambiguity aur gray-band handling ko project decision treat kiya jayega jab tak consolidated master document aur source archive koi zyada specific rule confirm na kar dein.

## Evidence and source notes

Available source anchor user-supplied specification hai, khas kar required Group E aur Group F curriculum entry is file ke liye. Named consolidated master document aur supporting attachments generation workspace mein present nahi the, isliye exact section names, citations, data-access facts, aur implementation status verify karne baaki hain. Ye file jaan-boojh ke unko manufacture nahi karti.

Requested source hierarchy hai: `SIH26079_MASTER_PROJECT_DOCUMENTATION_MERGED.md` as primary; original attachments aur archive for traceability and detail; verified references; aur completed master documentation. Extended library items research leads hi rahenge jab tak project records mein confirm na ho. Withdrawn Hurricane Michael article standing evidence nahi hai. Agar future revision IFS data-assimilation PDF par depend karega, to uske partial-extraction warning ko claim ke saath attached rehna chahiye.

## What is confirmed, what is proposed, and what is not yet verified

| Category | Treatment for this file |

| Confirmed by the available specification | Ye topic SIH26079 curriculum mein required hai aur explicit evidence labels ke saath handle karna zaroori hai. |
| Project decision or proposed rule | Upar describe kiya gaya topic-specific workflow aur safeguards. |
| Target / acceptance gate | Ek condition jo baad mein test ya document ki jayegi; ye result nahi hai. |
| Unverified / gap | Exact source wording, project implementation status, data access, empirical performance, aur koi bhi claim jo available specification mein present nahi hai. |
| Future / out of scope | Operational validation ya stronger scientific claims jinke liye naye data, permission, metadata, ya verification evidence chahiye honge. |

## Practical task or study exercise

Teen policies banao—exclude, soft-label, aur sensitivity-only—aur unke risks compare karo. Project default recommend tabhi karo jab master documentation intended label behavior confirm kar de.

Exercise ko ek chhota artifact ke roop mein complete karo: ek table, diagram, test case, protocol, ya decision record. Ek source column aur evidence-status column include karo. Agar koi named project source nahi mil raha, to **UNVERIFIED / GAP** likho aur item ko shared question register mein add karo instead of usko plausible assumption se fill karne ke.

## Questions to bring to the team discussion

1. Sabse chhota precise rule kya hai jo ensure karega ki yeh topic leakage, ambiguity, ya unsupported claim cause na kare?  
2. Kaunse fields, timestamps, versions, ya source sections auditability ke liye retain karne chahiye?  
3. Jab required evidence ya input missing ho to failure-safe behavior kya hai?  
4. Kaunsa aage ka experiment ya acceptance gate proposed rule ko confirm ya reject kar sakta hai?  
5. Kya koi wording by mistake imply kar rahi hai ki trained result, operational NCMRWF validation, causal explanation, ya universal guarantee hai?

## Deliverable or handoff after study

# File 057: Severity and continuous targets

**Assigned team member:** Member 3  
**Document type:** ML  
**Primary evidence label:** PROJECT DECISION

## Why this topic matters for SIH26079

Ek binary bust label yeh batata hai ki koi threshold cross hua tha ya nahi. Ek severity target koshish karta hai yeh preserve karne ki error reference level se kitna aage hai, jabki ek continuous target seedha error magnitude predict karta hai. Yeh alag-alag operational sawaalon ke liye hote hain aur inhe interchangeably treat nahi karna chahiye.

## Learning objective

Is brief ke end tak, team plain language mein topic explain kar sakni chahiye, identify kar sakni chahiye ki yeh kis project decision ya research question ko control karta hai, aur bata sakni chahiye ki stronger claim banane se pehle kaun sa evidence chahiye hoga. Yeh file ek focused internal study brief hai, published research paper nahi, aur yeh proof nahi hai ki implementation ya experiment complete hai.

## Key concepts in simple language

Target ke scale, units, censoring, aggregation, aur evaluation metric ko define karo. Ek continuous target ranking review priority ke liye useful ho sakta hai, lekin threshold crossing ki probability ek alag output hai aur uske liye calibration chahiye hoti hai. System ko expose karna chahiye ki kaunsa target ne prediction produce kiya aur bina documented contract ke binary aur continuous outputs mix nahi hone chahiye.

Key distinction hai availability, validity, aur use ke beech. Availability poochta hai kya koi value declared prediction time par exist karti hai. Validity poochta hai kya uska timestamp, units, coordinates, provenance, aur quality checks pass ho rahe hain. Use poochta hai kya project contract us value ko training, evaluation, replay, ya inference mein use karne ki permission deta hai. Ek value available ho sakti hai lekin invalid, valid ho sakti hai lekin kisi particular task ke liye forbidden, ya absent ho sakti hai aur isliye infer karna unsafe hai.

**Truth boundary.** Current project ek public-proxy prototype use kar raha hai jo GEFS ya WeatherBench 2 ke saath ERA5 reference analysis use karta hai. NCMRWF/NEPS operational validation future ka aur conditional hai jab tak paired historical data, metadata, permission, aur verification source actually provide nahi hote. Koi supplied material yeh establish nahi karta ki ek SIH26079 model ko train ya evaluate kiya gaya hai.

## Project-specific decisions or implementation rules

1. Issue time, valid time, lead time, dataset version, model version, aur provenance ko explicit fields ke roop mein preserve karo.
2. Jab required timestamp, source identifier, checksum, ya quality state missing ho to fail closed karo.
3. Public-proxy prototype ko kisi future operational NCMRWF/NEPS pathway se separate rakho.
4. Targets aur acceptance gates ko achieved measurements se visibly distinct rakho.
5. Topic ko prerequisite files se link karo na ki unka content is brief mein copy karo.

Is file ke liye working decision yeh hai ki **severity and continuous targets** ko **project decision** maana jaaye jab tak consolidated master document aur source archive koi specific rule confirm nahi kar dete.

# File 058: Spatial displacement and neighborhood/object labels

**Assigned team member:** Member 3  
**Document type:** research  
**Primary evidence label:** UNVERIFIED / GAP

## Why this topic matters for SIH26079

Ek forecast galat ho sakta hai kyunki ek event misplaced, mistimed, bahut broad, bahut narrow, ya missing ho sakta hai. Ek pointwise error threshold in differences ko achchhi tarah capture nahi karta. Spatial ya object-based labels neighborhoods, coherent structures, ya matched features ko compare kar sakte hain, lekin inke liye explicit spatial matching rules aur verified source support chahiye.

## Learning objective

Is brief ke end tak, team plain language mein topic samjha sakni chahiye, identify kar sakni chahiye ki kaunsa project decision ya research question isse control hota hai, aur bata sakni chahiye ki stronger claim karne se pehle kaunsa evidence chahiye hoga. Ye file ek focused internal study brief hai, published research paper nahi, aur na hi ye proof hai ki implementation ya experiment complete hai.

## Key concepts in simple language

Aise labels implement karne se pehle object, neighborhood radius, matching tolerance, grid treatment, aur multiple objects ka treatment define karo. ERA5 reference analysis ek reference resource hi rahega aur automatically perfect truth provide nahi karta. Spatial labels ko pointwise labels se alag version karna chahiye kyunki ye alag sawalon ka jawab dete hain.

Key distinction ye hai: availability, validity, aur use. Availability poochta hai kya koi value declared prediction time par exist karti hai. Validity poochta hai kya uska timestamp, units, coordinates, provenance, aur quality checks pass karte hain. Use poochta hai kya project contract us value ko training, evaluation, replay, ya inference mein use karne ki permission deta hai. Ek value available ho sakti hai lekin invalid; valid ho sakti hai lekin kisi particular task ke liye forbidden; ya absent ho sakti hai aur isliye infer karna unsafe hai.

**Truth boundary.** Current project ek public-proxy prototype use kar raha hai GEFS ya WeatherBench 2 ke saath ERA5 reference analysis. NCMRWF/NEPS operational validation future aur conditional hai jab tak paired historical data, metadata, permission, aur verification source actually provide nahi kiye jaate. Koi supplied material establish nahi karta ki SIH26079 model ko train ya evaluate kiya gaya hai.

**Causality rule.** Koi bhi feature association ya disagreement pattern correlational evidence hoga, causal proof nahi.

## Project-specific decisions or implementation rules

1. Preserve issue time, valid time, lead time, dataset version, model version, aur provenance ko explicit fields ke roop mein rakho.  
2. Jab required timestamp, source identifier, checksum, ya quality state missing ho toh fail closed karo.  
3. Public-proxy prototype ko kisi future operational NCMRWF/NEPS pathway se separate rakho.  
4. Targets aur acceptance gates ko achieved measurements se visibly distinct rakho.  
5. Topic ko prerequisite files se link karo instead of unke content ko is brief mein copy karne ke.

# File 059: Rare-event class imbalance

**Assigned team member:** Member 3  
**Document type:** ML  
**Primary evidence label:** FROM LITERATURE

## Why this topic matters for SIH26079

Rare busts non-busts ke mukable kam frequent ho sakte hain, khaaskar high thresholds par. Class imbalance training, threshold selection, uncertainty estimates, aur metric interpretation ko affect karta hai. Accuracy high dikh sakti hai even jab detector adhiktar positive cases miss kar raha ho, isliye evaluation plan mein event-focused measures aur prevalence report karna zaroori hai.

## Learning objective

Is brief ke ant tak, team plain language mein topic samjha sakni chahiye, identify kar sakni chahiye ki yeh kaunsa project decision ya research question control karta hai, aur yeh bata sakni chahiye ki strong claim karne se pehle kaunsa evidence chahiye hoga. Yeh file ek focused internal study brief hai, published research paper nahi, aur na hi yeh proof hai ki implementation ya experiment complete hai.

## Key concepts in simple language

Split aur important strata mein prevalence alag se measure karo. Split se pehle oversample na karo aur partitions mein duplicated event records allow na karo. Agar class weights ya resampling test kiye gaye hain, to unko experiment factors ke roop mein record karo. Precision prevalence par depend karti hai, isliye ek sample ka result universal operating characteristic ke roop mein present nahi kia ja sakta.

Key distinction **availability**, **validity**, aur **use** ke beech hai. Availability poochti hai ki kya ek value declared prediction time par exist karti hai. Validity poochti hai ki kya uska timestamp, units, coordinates, provenance, aur quality checks pass karte hain. Use poochta hai ki kya project contract us value ko training, evaluation, replay, ya inference mein allow karta hai. Ek value available ho sakti hai lekin invalid, valid ho sakti hai lekin kisi khas task ke liye forbidden, ya absent ho sakti hai aur isliye infer karna unsafe hoga.

**Truth boundary.** Current project ek public-proxy prototype hai jo GEFS ya WeatherBench 2 use karta hai with ERA5 reference analysis. NCMRWF/NEPS operational validation future aur conditional hai jab tak paired historical data, metadata, permission, aur verification source actually provide na ho. Koi supplied material yeh establish nahi karta ki ek SIH26079 model train ya evaluate kia gaya hai.

**Result-status rule.** Koi bhi numeric comparison, prevalence, threshold choice, ya metric value later experiment record mein belong karti hai. Is brief mein koi SIH26079 result nahi hai.

## Project-specific decisions or implementation rules

1. Preserve issue time, valid time, lead time, dataset version, model version, aur provenance as explicit fields.  
2. Fail closed jab required timestamp, source identifier, checksum, ya quality state missing ho.  
3. Public-proxy prototype ko kisi bhi future operational NCMRWF/NEPS pathway se alag rakho.  
4. Targets aur acceptance gates ko achieved measurements se visibly distinct rakho.  
5. Topic ko prerequisite files se link karo instead of unka content is brief mein copy karne ke.

Is file ke liye working decision yeh hai ki **rare-event class imbalance** ko **from literature** treat kiya jayega jab tak consolidated master document aur source archive kisi zyada specific rule ko confirm na karen.

## Evidence and source notes

Available source anchor user-supplied specification hai, khaaskar is file ke liye required Group E aur Group F curriculum entry. # File 060: Label-versioning and reproducibility

**Assigned team member:** Member 3  
**Document type:** governance  
**Primary evidence label:** TARGET / ACCEPTANCE GATE

## Why this topic matters for SIH26079

Label definitions tabdeel ho sakti hain jab error measure, threshold, alignment rule, gray band, spatial scope, ya exclusion policy change hoti hai. Agar in changes ko versioned nahi kiya gaya to experiment reproduce nahi ho paayega aur model comparison ambiguous ho jayega. Isliye ek label version model input contract ka hissa hona chahiye.

## Learning objective

Is brief ke end tak, team plain language mein topic explain kar paaye, identify kar paaye ki kis project decision ya research question ko yeh control karta hai, aur bata paaye ki stronger claim karne se pehle kis evidence ki zarurat hogi. Yeh file ek focused internal study brief hai, published research paper nahi, aur na hi yeh proof hai ke implementation ya experiment complete hai.

## Key concepts in simple language

Use karo ek stable label ID plus ek human-readable changelog. Store karo source dataset versions, code revision, parameters, split policy, aur generation timestamp. Ek naya label version compatibility review trigger kare features, baselines, metrics, calibration, aur dashboard copy ke liye. Kabhi bhi pehle waali label definition ko inplace overwrite mat karo.

Key distinction yeh hai between **availability**, **validity**, aur **use**. Availability poochta hai kya koi value declared prediction time par exist karti hai. Validity poochta hai kya uska timestamp, units, coordinates, provenance, aur quality checks pass karte hain. Use poochta hai kya project contract value ko training, evaluation, replay, ya inference mein allow karta hai. Ek value available ho sakti hai lekin invalid, valid ho sakti hai lekin kisi particular task ke liye forbidden, ya absent ho sakti hai aur isliye infer karna unsafe hai.

**Truth boundary.** Current project ek public-proxy prototype hai jo GEFS ya WeatherBench 2 ke saath ERA5 reference analysis use karta hai. NCMRWF/NEPS operational validation future mein conditional hai jab tak paired historical data, metadata, permission, aur ek verification source waqayi provide nahi kiya jata. Koi supplied material yeh establish nahi karta ki SIH26079 model train ya evaluate hua hai.

**Implementation caution.** Specification dwara named source documents ko check karna zaroori hai pehle koi proposed threshold, schema, ya status ko implemented project fact maan ne se pehle.

## Project-specific decisions or implementation rules

1. Preserve karo issue time, valid time, lead time, dataset version, model version, aur provenance ko explicit fields ke roop mein.  
2. Fail closed jab required timestamp, source identifier, checksum, ya quality state missing ho.  
3. Public-proxy prototype ko separate rakho kisi bhi future operational NCMRWF/NEPS pathway se.  
4. Targets aur acceptance gates ko visibly distinct rakho from achieved measurements.  
5. Topic ko prerequisite files se link karo zami ke unka content is brief mein copy karo.

Is file ke liye working decision yeh hai ke **label-versioning and reproducibility** ko treat kiya jaaye as **target / acceptance gate** jab tak consolidated master document aur source archive koi zyada specific rule confirm nahi kar dete.

## Evidence and source notes

Available source anchor user-supplied specification hai, khaaskar required Group E aur Group F curriculum entry jo is file ke liye zaroori hai. Named consolidated master document aur supporting attachments generation workspace mein maujood nahi the, isliye exact section names, citations, data-access facts, aur implementation status verify karne baaki hain. Yeh file jaan-boojh ke un cheezon ko manufacture nahi karti.

Requested source hierarchy yeh hai: `SIH26079_MASTER_PROJECT_DOCUMENTATION_MERGED.md` as primary; original attachments aur archive for traceability aur detail; verified references; aur completed master documentation. Extended library items research leads hi rahenge jab tak project records mein confirm na ho. Withdrawn Hurricane Michael article standing evidence nahi hai. Agar future revision IFS data-assimilation PDF par depend kare to uska partial-extraction warning claim ke saath attached rehna chahiye.

## What is confirmed, what is proposed, and what is not yet verified

| Category | Treatment for this file |
|---|---|
| Confirmed by the available specification | This topic is required in the SIH26079 curriculum and must be handled with explicit evidence labels. |
| Project decision or proposed rule | The topic-specific workflow and safeguards described above. |
| Target / acceptance gate | A condition that must be tested or documented later; it is not a result. |
| Unverified / gap | Exact source wording, project implementation status, data access, empirical performance, and any claim not present in the available specification. |
| Future / out of scope | Operational validation or stronger scientific claims requiring new data, permission, metadata, or verification evidence. |

## Practical task or study exercise

Draft karo ek changelog entry for changing q95 to q97.5, changing a stale-data rule, aur adding a gray band. Har entry mein identify karo kya old labels reuse kiye ja sakte hain aur kaunse experiments rerun karne honge.

Complete karo exercise as a small artifact: ek table, diagram, test case, protocol, ya decision record. Include karo ek source column aur ek evidence-status column. Agar koi named project source nahi milta to likho **UNVERIFIED / GAP** aur item ko shared question register mein daal do instead of usko plausible assumption se fill karne ke.

## Questions to bring to the team discussion

1. Sabse chhota precise rule kya hai jo ensure kare ke yeh topic leakage, ambiguity, ya unsupported claim cause na kare?  
2. Kaunse fields, timestamps, versions, ya source sections retain karne chahiye for auditability?  
3. Jab required evidence ya input missing ho to failure-safe behaviour kya hoga?  
4. Kaunsa later experiment ya acceptance gate proposed rule ko confirm ya reject kar sakta hai?  
5. Kya koi wording galti se imply karti hai ki trained result, operational NCMRWF validation, causal explanation, ya universal guarantee hai?

## Deliverable or handoff after study

Hand off karo completed exercise, ek three-sentence conclusion, evidence label, exact source location agar baad mein verified ho, aur ek unresolved question. Handoff aisa hona chahiye ke next dependent file bina pura topic dobara padhe usko use kar sake.

**Assigned team member:** Member 3

## Why this topic matters for SIH26079

Ensemble geometry batata hai ki members ek‑dusre ke around kaise vary karte hain. Candidate quantities mein spread, pairwise disagreement, rank structure, aur distance from an ensemble center shaamil ho sakte hain. Ye quantities forecast uncertainty ya diversity ko describe karti hain; ye prove nahi karti ki koi bust hoga. Missing members, unequal weighting, aur changing member counts same formula ko records ke across incomparable bana sakte hain.

## Learning objective

Is brief ke end tak, team plain language mein topic explain kar paani chahiye, identify kar paani chahiye ki kaunsa project decision ya research question isse control hota hai, aur bata paani chahiye ki stronger claim karne se pehle kaunsa evidence chahiye hoga. ## Key concepts in simple language

Freeze karo ensemble membership rule, center calculation, units, spatial aggregation, aur minimum completeness. Raw counts aur quality flags ko derived values ke saath saath rakho. Ek spread-only baseline ko test karna chahiye before ensemble geometry ko bahut saare doosre features ke saath combine karne se, taaki team bata sake ki complexity evidence add kar rahi hai ya nahi.

Key distinction availability, validity, aur use ke beech hai. Availability poochti hai kya koi value declared prediction time par exist karti hai. Validity poochti hai kya uska timestamp, units, coordinates, provenance, aur quality checks pass karte hain. Use poochta hai kya project contract us value ko training, evaluation, replay, ya inference mein allow karta hai. Ek value available ho sakti hai par invalid, valid ho sakti hai par kisi particular task ke liye forbidden, ya absent ho sakti hai aur isliye unsafe to infer.

**Truth boundary.** Current project ek public-proxy prototype hai jo GEFS ya WeatherBench 2 ko ERA5 reference analysis ke saath use karta hai. NCMRWF/NEPS operational validation future aur conditional hai jab tak paired historical data, metadata, permission, aur verification source actually provide nahi kiye jaate. Koi supplied material yeh establish nahi karta ki ek SIH26079 model ko train ya evaluate kiya gaya hai.

1. Preserve karo issue time, valid time, lead time, dataset version, model version, aur provenance ko explicit fields ke roop mein.  
2. Fail closed jab required timestamp, source identifier, checksum, ya quality state missing ho.  
3. Public-proxy prototype ko kisi bhi future operational NCMRWF/NEPS pathway se alag rakho.  
4. Targets aur acceptance gates ko visibly distinct rakho from achieved measurements.

Ek trajectory feature yeh summarize karta hai ki forecast quantities kaise evolve karte hain across lead times ya cycles. Yeh instability, rapid revision, ya growing disagreement dikhaa sakta hai. Kyunki aggregation timing ko chhupa sakti hai, feature definition mein clear likhna zaroori hai ki kaunse trajectory points available hain aur missing lead times ko kaise handle kiya jayega.

## Learning objective

Is brief ke end tak, team ko ye kar paana chahiye: topic ko plain language mein explain karna, identify karna ki kaunsa project decision ya research question is topic se control hota hai, aur batana ki stronger claim karne se pehle kaunse evidence chahiye. Ye file ek focused internal study brief hai — published research paper nahi, aur na hi ye evidence hai ki implementation ya experiment complete hua hai.

## Key concepts in simple language

Trajectory domain, ordering key, interpolation policy, summary statistic, aur provenance ko define karo. Ensemble members ke across divergence ko forecast cycles ke across divergence se alag rakho; dono alag signals hain. Ek trajectory feature interpretable rehna chahiye taaki later ablation aur explanation possible ho, bina kisi causal mechanism imply kiye.

Key distinction ye hai: availability, validity, aur use. Availability puchta hai kya koi value declared prediction time par exist karti hai. Validity puchta hai kya uska timestamp, units, coordinates, provenance, aur quality checks pass karte hain. Use puchta hai kya project contract us value ko training, evaluation, replay, ya inference mein allow karta hai. Ek value available ho sakti hai par invalid; valid ho sakti hai par kisi particular task ke liye forbidden; ya absent ho sakti hai aur isliye unsafe to infer.

**Truth boundary.** Current project ek public-proxy prototype hai jo GEFS ya WeatherBench 2 ko ERA5 reference analysis ke saath use karta hai. NCMRWF/NEPS operational validation future ka aur conditional hai jab tak paired historical data, metadata, permission, aur verification source waastav mein provide na ho. Koi bhi supplied material is baat ko establish nahi karta ki ek SIH26079 model train ya evaluate hua hai.

## Project-specific decisions or implementation rules

1. Preserve issue time, valid time, lead time, dataset version, model version, aur provenance as explicit fields.
2. Fail closed jab bhi ek required timestamp, source identifier, checksum, ya quality state missing ho.
3. Public-proxy prototype ko kisi bhi future operational NCMRWF/NEPS pathway se separate rakho.
4. Targets aur acceptance gates ko visibly distinct rakho from achieved measurements.
5. Topic ko prerequisite files se link karo zamiin ke is brief mein unka content copy karne ke.

Define analog retrieval as a contextual feature, not causal evidence. The expected result is a traceable understanding of the topic, not a claim that an implementation, experiment, or operational validation already exists.

**Truth boundary.** The specification requires the current project to be treated as a public-proxy prototype using GEFS or WeatherBench 2 with ERA5 reference analysis. NCMRWF/NEPS operational validation is future and conditional until paired historical data, metadata, permission, and a verification source are actually provided. No supplied material establishes that a SIH26079 model has been trained or evaluated.

**Interpretation caveat.** SHAP, feature importance, and analog retrieval are correlational evidence or similarity evidence, not causal proof.

## Key concepts in simple language

A **research study file** is an internal learning brief. It may summarize confirmed literature, record a project decision, define a target or acceptance gate, expose an unverified gap, or describe future work. These categories are not interchangeable. A target is a condition to test later. A project decision is a chosen design rule. A gap is information the team still needs. A future item is outside the current demonstrated scope.

For this topic, the team should begin by asking what is observable in the supplied records, what is only proposed, and what would require a new source or experiment. The safe default is to preserve uncertainty rather than fill it with a plausible-sounding detail. A source name in the specification is a lead unless the project records confirm the exact statement.

Extended-library items remain research leads unless confirmed in project records. The withdrawn Hurricane Michael article must not be used as standing evidence.
